 469:     private ReplayDataset loadDataset(String symbol, String btcSymbol, Instant start, Instant end) {
 470:         Map<ReplayDataset.CandleKey, List<Candle>> candleMap = new HashMap<>();
 471:         Set<String> symbols = new LinkedHashSet<>(List.of(symbol, btcSymbol));
 472:         int historyLimit = technicalIndicatorService.regressionHistoryLimit();
 473:         for (String sym : symbols) {
 474:             for (String interval : List.of("1m", "5m", "1h")) {
 475:                 List<Candle> prefix = new java.util.ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 476:                         sym, interval, start, PageRequest.of(0, historyLimit)));
 477:                 java.util.Collections.reverse(prefix);
 478:                 List<Candle> forward = candleRepository
 479:                         .findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 480:                                 sym, interval, start, end);
 481:                 List<Candle> merged = new java.util.ArrayList<>(prefix.size() + forward.size());
 482:                 merged.addAll(prefix);
 483:                 Instant prefixEnd = prefix.isEmpty() ? Instant.EPOCH : prefix.get(prefix.size() - 1).getOpenTime();
 484:                 forward.stream().filter(c -> c.getOpenTime().isAfter(prefixEnd)).forEach(merged::add);
 485:                 candleMap.put(new ReplayDataset.CandleKey(sym, interval), merged);
 486:                 log.info("FIX-11H dataset candles: symbol={}, interval={}, prefix={}, forward={}, merged={}",
 487:                         sym, interval, prefix.size(), forward.size(), merged.size());
 488:             }
 489:         }
 490: 
 491:         Map<ReplayDataset.SignalKey, Long> lineage = new HashMap<>();
 492:         // FIX-11H / FIX-112D: lineage is candle identity, not signal persistence timing.
 493:         // Recovery/backfilled Production signals can be generated hours after their candle, so
 494:         // generatedAt MUST NOT define this preload window. Select and match lineage by the same
 495:         // exact candleOpenTime identity used by ReplayDataset.sourceSignalId().
 496:         for (TradeSignal signal : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndCandleOpenTimeBetweenOrderByCandleOpenTimeAsc(symbol, start, end)) {
 497:             ReplayDataset.SignalKey key = new ReplayDataset.SignalKey(
 498:                     signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), signal.getCandleOpenTime());
 499:             Long previous = lineage.putIfAbsent(key, signal.getId());
 500:             if (previous != null && !previous.equals(signal.getId())) {
 501:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Replay lineage invariant violated for " + key
 502:                         + ": signalIds=" + previous + "," + signal.getId());
 503:             }
 504:         }
 505:         log.info("FIX-11H dataset lineage: symbol={}, entries={}", symbol, lineage.size());
 506:         return new ReplayDataset(candleMap, lineage);
 507:     }
 508: 
 509:     private List<Candle> candles(String symbol, String interval, Instant start, Instant end) {
 510:         return candleRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 511:                 symbol, interval, start, end);
 512:     }
 513: 
 514:     private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
 515:                                       int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
 516:         int resolved = 0;
 517:         int size = candles.size();
 518:         for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
 519:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 520:             Candle candle = candles.get(i);
 521:             List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 522:                     symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
 523:             if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
 524:                 resolved++;
 525:             }
 526:             if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
 527:                 int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
 528:                 updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
 529:                         "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
 530:             }
 531:         }
 532:         return resolved;
 533:     }
 534: 
 535:     private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
 536:             long runId,
 537:             String symbol,
 538:             Instant executionStart,
 539:             Instant executionEnd,
 540:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
 541:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
 542:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
 543:             String btcSymbol,
 544:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
 545:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
 546:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
 547:             ReplayDataSource dataSource,
 548:             ReplayDataset replayDataset
 549:     ) {
 550:         List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 551:         oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
 552:         fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
 554:         btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
 557:         timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
 558:                 .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
 559:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
 560:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
 561: 
 562:         int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
 563:         int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
 564:         int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
 565:         java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 566: 
 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
 570:         ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
 571:         // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
 572:         // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
 573:         analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 574:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
 581:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 582:             ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
 583:             Candle candle = replay.candle();
 584:             Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
 585:             boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 591:                             ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 592:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606: 
 607:                 // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
 608:                 // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
 609:                 // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
 610:                 if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
 611: 
 612:                 replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
 613:                 operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 614:                 TradeSignal generated;
 615:                 try {
 616:                     generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
 617:                 } finally {
 618:                     profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 619:                 }
 620:                 replayScope.appendSignal(generated);
 621:                 SignalDecision decision = generated.getDecision();
 622:                 if (replay.primary()) generatedSignals.add(generated);
 623: 
 624:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 625:                     if ("1m".equals(replay.interval())) { oneMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneMinuteBuys++; }
 626:                     else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }
 627:                     else if ("1h".equals(replay.interval())) { oneHourSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneHourBuys++; }
 628: 
 629:                     if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) buys++;
 630:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
 631:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) sells++;
 632:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) strongSells++;
 633: 
 634:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 635:                     try {
 636:                         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 637:                             INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
 638:                                 (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
 639:                                  generated_at, latest_price, original_decision, final_decision,
 640:                                  execution_effective_decision, total_score, confidence_score, raw_confidence_score,
 641:                                  effective_confidence_score, primary_blocking_stage, detected_regime, candidate_regime,
 642:                                  confirmed_regime, regime_candidate_count, entry_authority, entry_authority_max_position_percent,
 643:                                  trend_score, volume_score, momentum_score, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
 644:                             VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, NULL)
 645:                             """,
 646:                             runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
 647:                             Timestamp.from(evaluationTime), generated.getLatestPrice(),
 648:                             generated.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : generated.getOriginalDecision().name(),
 649:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 650:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 651:                             generated.getTotalScore(), generated.getConfidenceScore(), generated.getRawConfidenceScore(),
 652:                             generated.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), generated.getPrimaryBlockingStage(),
 653:                             generated.getDetectedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getDetectedRegime().name(),
 654:                             generated.getCandidateRegime() == null ? null : generated.getCandidateRegime().name(),
 655:                             generated.getConfirmedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getConfirmedRegime().name(),
 656:                             generated.getRegimeCandidateCount(), generated.getEntryAuthority(),
 657:                             generated.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), generated.getTrendScore(),
 658:                             generated.getVolumeScore(), generated.getMomentumScore());
 659:                     } finally {
 660:                         profiler.recordAnalysisTestSignalPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 661:                     }
 662: 
 663:                     // FIX-069: Persist the exact production TradeSignal shape as replay output.
 664:                     // analysis_test_signal remains for backward-compatible diagnostics, while
 665:                     // trade_signal_test is the canonical parity table used for field-by-field comparison.
 666:                     // FIX-112D: Replay lineage uses exact market-candle identity, never
 667:                     // generated_at or nearest-time matching. A legitimate Replay-only
 668:                     // signal remains NULL. This metadata lookup cannot alter Production.
 669:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 670:                     Long sourceSignalId;
 671:                     try {
 672:                         sourceSignalId = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 673:                                 ? replayDataset.sourceSignalId(
 674:                                         generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
 675:                                 : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
 676:                                         generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
 677:                                         .map(TradeSignal::getId).orElse(null);
 678:                     } finally {
 679:                         profiler.recordLineageLookup(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 680:                     }
 681:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 682:                     try {
 683:                         persistTradeSignalTest(runId, generated, sourceSignalId, null);
 684:                     } finally {
 685:                         profiler.recordTradeSignalTestPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 686:                     }
 687:                 }
 688:             } catch (ReplayCancellationException stop) {
 689:                 throw stop;
 690:             } catch (Exception exception) {
 691:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 692:                     errors++;
 693:                     saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle, errorDetail(exception, 900));
 694:                 }
 695:                 log.warn("Regression fresh-signal generation failed: run={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candle={}",
 696:                         runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), exception);
 697:             }
 698: 
 699:             if (index % 10 == 0 && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
 700:                 int progress = 43 + (int) Math.round(30d * index / timeline.size());

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java (700-950) =====
 700:                 int progress = 43 + (int) Math.round(30d * index / timeline.size());
 701:                 long progressStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 702:                 try {
 703:                     updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", Math.min(73, progress),
 704:                             "Generating fresh signal " + (index + 1) + "/" + timeline.size(), null);
 705:                 } finally {
 706:                     profiler.recordProgressUpdate(System.nanoTime() - progressStartedNs);
 707:                 }
 708:             }
 709:         }
 710: 
 711:         } finally {
 712:             // FIX-11M must never turn a valid Replay into ERROR if diagnostic summary logging fails.
 713:             try {
 714:                 analysisService.finishReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 715:             } catch (Exception profilingException) {
 716:                 log.warn("FIX11M_REPLAY_ANALYSIS_PROFILE_ERROR run={} message={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false",
 717:                         runId, profilingException.getMessage());
 718:             }
 719:             profiler.logSummary(log, timeline.size());
 720:         }
 721:         return new FreshReplayStats(oneMinuteSignals, oneMinuteBuys, fiveMinuteSignals, fiveMinuteBuys,
 722:                 oneHourSignals, oneHourBuys, buys, watches, sells, strongSells, errors, generatedSignals);
 723:     }

 725:     private void saveGenerationError(long runId, String symbol, String interval, Candle candle, String error) {
 726:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 727:                 INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
 728:                     (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
 729:                      generated_at, latest_price, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
 730:                 VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?)
 731:                 """, runId, symbol, interval, Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
 732:                 Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval))),
 733:                 candle.getClosePrice(), error);
 734:     }
 735: 
 736:     /**
 737:      * FIX-069: write every field mapped by the TradeSignal JPA entity into trade_signal_test.
 738:      * Reflection is intentional here: whenever production TradeSignal gains another @Column,
 739:      * replay inherits it automatically instead of drifting behind a hand-maintained test schema.
 740:      */
 741:     private void persistTradeSignalTest(long runId, TradeSignal signal, Long sourceSignalId, String generationError) {
 742:         try {
 743:             java.util.List<String> columns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 744:             java.util.List<Object> values = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 745:             columns.add("test_run_id"); values.add(runId);
 746:             // FIX-112D / Replay=Production audit lineage: this ID points only to the
 747:             // exact Production signal for the same symbol + interval + candle_open_time.
 748:             // It is additive Replay metadata and is never an execution authority.
 749:             columns.add("source_signal_id"); values.add(sourceSignalId);
 750:             columns.add("replay_generated"); values.add(1);
 751:             columns.add("generation_error"); values.add(generationError);
 752: 
 753:             for (Field field : TradeSignal.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
 754:                 if ("id".equals(field.getName()) || "createdAt".equals(field.getName())) continue;
 755:                 Column column = field.getAnnotation(Column.class);
 756:                 if (column == null) continue;
 757:                 field.setAccessible(true);
 758:                 Object value = field.get(signal);
 759:                 if (value instanceof Enum<?> e) value = e.name();
 760:                 if (value instanceof Instant instant) value = Timestamp.from(instant);
 761:                 String columnName = column.name();
 762:                 if (columnName == null || columnName.isBlank()) columnName = snakeCase(field.getName());
 763:                 columns.add(columnName);
 764:                 values.add(value);
 765:             }
 766: 
 767:             String placeholders = String.join(",", java.util.Collections.nCopies(values.size(), "?"));
 768:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test (" + String.join(",", columns) + ") VALUES (" + placeholders + ")", values.toArray());
 769:         } catch (Exception e) {
 770:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
 771:         }
 772:     }
 773: 
 774:     private String snakeCase(String value) {
 775:         return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
 776:     }
 777: 
 778:     private long intervalSeconds(String interval) {
 779:         return switch (interval) {
 780:             case "1m" -> 60L;
 781:             case "5m" -> 300L;
 782:             case "1h" -> 3600L;
 783:             default -> 60L;
 784:         };
 785:     }
 786: 
 787:     private int intervalOrder(String interval) {
 788:         return switch (interval) {
 789:             case "1h" -> 0;
 790:             case "5m" -> 1;
 791:             default -> 2;
 792:         };
 793:     }
 794: 
 795:     private boolean isBuy(SignalDecision decision) {
 796:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 797:     }

 799:     /**
 800:      * FIX-11L Replay-only profiler for the fresh-signal generation loop. It deliberately
 801:      * aggregates timings in memory and emits one summary log after the stage; there are no
 802:      * per-signal log writes and no Production/shared-analysis branches.
 803:      */
 804:     private static final class ReplayFreshSignalProfiler {
 805:         private final long runId;
 806:         private final ReplayDataSource dataSource;
 807:         private final long startedNs = System.nanoTime();
 808:         private long snapshotNs;
 809:         private long analysisNs;
 810:         private long analysisTestSignalPersistenceNs;
 811:         private long lineageLookupNs;
 812:         private long tradeSignalTestPersistenceNs;
 813:         private long progressUpdateNs;
 814:         private long snapshotCalls;
 815:         private long analysisCalls;
 816:         private long analysisTestSignalPersistenceCalls;
 817:         private long lineageLookupCalls;
 818:         private long tradeSignalTestPersistenceCalls;
 819:         private long progressUpdateCalls;
 820:         private long maxSnapshotNs;
 821:         private long maxAnalysisNs;
 822: 
 823:         private ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(long runId, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
 824:             this.runId = runId;
 825:             this.dataSource = dataSource;
 826:         }
 827: 
 828:         private void recordSnapshot(long elapsedNs) {
 829:             snapshotNs += elapsedNs;
 830:             snapshotCalls++;
 831:             maxSnapshotNs = Math.max(maxSnapshotNs, elapsedNs);
 832:         }
 833: 
 834:         private void recordAnalysis(long elapsedNs) {
 835:             analysisNs += elapsedNs;
 836:             analysisCalls++;
 837:             maxAnalysisNs = Math.max(maxAnalysisNs, elapsedNs);
 838:         }
 839: 
 840:         private void recordAnalysisTestSignalPersistence(long elapsedNs) {
 841:             analysisTestSignalPersistenceNs += elapsedNs;
 842:             analysisTestSignalPersistenceCalls++;
 843:         }
 844: 
 845:         private void recordLineageLookup(long elapsedNs) {
 846:             lineageLookupNs += elapsedNs;
 847:             lineageLookupCalls++;
 848:         }
 849: 
 850:         private void recordTradeSignalTestPersistence(long elapsedNs) {
 851:             tradeSignalTestPersistenceNs += elapsedNs;
 852:             tradeSignalTestPersistenceCalls++;
 853:         }
 854: 
 855:         private void recordProgressUpdate(long elapsedNs) {
 856:             progressUpdateNs += elapsedNs;
 857:             progressUpdateCalls++;
 858:         }
 859: 
 860:         private void logSummary(org.slf4j.Logger logger, int timelineSize) {
 861:             long totalNs = System.nanoTime() - startedNs;
 862:             long measuredNs = snapshotNs + analysisNs + analysisTestSignalPersistenceNs
 863:                     + lineageLookupNs + tradeSignalTestPersistenceNs + progressUpdateNs;
 864:             long otherNs = Math.max(0L, totalNs - measuredNs);
 865:             logger.info(
 866:                     "FIX11L_REPLAY_SIGNAL_PROFILE run={} dataSource={} timeline={} totalMs={} "
 867:                             + "snapshotMs={} snapshotCalls={} snapshotAvgMs={} snapshotMaxMs={} "
 868:                             + "analysisMs={} analysisCalls={} analysisAvgMs={} analysisMaxMs={} "
 869:                             + "analysisTestSignalPersistMs={} analysisTestSignalPersistCalls={} "
 870:                             + "lineageMs={} lineageCalls={} tradeSignalTestPersistMs={} tradeSignalTestPersistCalls={} "
 871:                             + "progressMs={} progressCalls={} otherMs={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false",
 872:                     runId, dataSource, timelineSize, millis(totalNs),
 873:                     millis(snapshotNs), snapshotCalls, averageMillis(snapshotNs, snapshotCalls), millis(maxSnapshotNs),
 874:                     millis(analysisNs), analysisCalls, averageMillis(analysisNs, analysisCalls), millis(maxAnalysisNs),
 875:                     millis(analysisTestSignalPersistenceNs), analysisTestSignalPersistenceCalls,
 876:                     millis(lineageLookupNs), lineageLookupCalls, millis(tradeSignalTestPersistenceNs),
 877:                     tradeSignalTestPersistenceCalls, millis(progressUpdateNs), progressUpdateCalls, millis(otherNs));
 878:         }
 879: 
 880:         private static String millis(long ns) {
 881:             return String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.3f", ns / 1_000_000.0d);
 882:         }
 883: 
 884:         private static String averageMillis(long ns, long calls) {
 885:             return calls == 0L ? "0.000"
 886:                     : String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.3f", (ns / 1_000_000.0d) / calls);
 887:         }
 888:     }

 890:     private record ReplayCandle(String symbol, String interval, Candle candle, boolean primary) {}

 892:     private record FreshReplayStats(
 893:             int oneMinuteSignals, int oneMinuteBuys,
 894:             int fiveMinuteSignals, int fiveMinuteBuys,
 895:             int oneHourSignals, int oneHourBuys,
 896:             int buys, int watches, int sells, int strongSells, int errors,
 897:             java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals
 898:     ) {
 899:         int total() { return oneMinuteSignals + fiveMinuteSignals + oneHourSignals; }
 900:     }

 902:     private List<Candle> candlesInExecutionWindow(List<Candle> candles, String interval, Instant start, Instant end) {
 903:         return candles.stream()
 904:                 .filter(c -> {
 905:                     Instant t = c.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval));
 906:                     return !t.isBefore(start) && !t.isAfter(end);
 907:                 })
 908:                 .toList();
 909:     }
 910: 
 911:     private int countSignals(List<TradeSignal> signals, String interval) {
 912:         return (int) signals.stream().filter(s -> interval.equals(s.getInterval())).count();
 913:     }

 915:     private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 916:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 917:     }
 918: 
 919:     private void updateRun(long runId, String status, int progress, String step, String error) {
 920:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 921:                 UPDATE analysis_test_run
 922:                 SET status=?, progress_percent=?, current_step=?, error_message=?,
 923:                     started_at=COALESCE(started_at, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)), heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 924:                 WHERE id=?
 925:                 """, status, Math.max(0, Math.min(100, progress)), step, error, runId);
 926:     }
 927:

 930:     private String currentStep(long runId) {
 931:         try {
 932:             return jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
 933:                     "SELECT current_step FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id = ?", String.class, runId);
 934:         } catch (Exception ignored) {
 935:             return "Unknown regression phase";
 936:         }
 937:     }
 938: 
 939:     private Throwable rootCause(Throwable exception) {
 940:         if (exception == null) return null;
 941:         Throwable root = exception;
 942:         while (root.getCause() != null && root.getCause() != root) {
 943:             root = root.getCause();
 944:         }
 945:         return root;
 946:     }
 947: 
 948:     private String rootCauseDetail(Throwable root, int max) {
 949:         if (root == null) return "Unknown root cause";
 950:         String message = root.getMessage();

 177:     /** FIX-11H explicit replay-only OLD/NEW selector. Production is not configurable through this mode. */
 178:     public synchronized long start(RegressionTestRunRequest request, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
 179:         if (request == null || request.symbol() == null || request.symbol().isBlank()
 180:                 || request.startTime() == null || request.endTime() == null
 181:                 || !request.endTime().isAfter(request.startTime())) {
 182:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol, start time and end time are required, and end must be after start.");
 183:         }
 184: 
 185:         Integer activeRuns = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("""
 186:                 SELECT COUNT(*)
 187:                 FROM analysis_test_run
 188:                 WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
 189:                 """, Integer.class);
 190:         if (activeRuns != null && activeRuns > 0) {
 191:             Map<String, Object> active = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap("""
 192:                     SELECT id, test_name, symbol, status, progress_percent, started_at, created_at
 193:                     FROM analysis_test_run
 194:                     WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
 195:                     ORDER BY id DESC
 196:                     LIMIT 1
 197:                     """);
 198:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
 199:                     "Regression test #" + active.get("id") + " is already " + active.get("status")
 200:                             + " for " + active.get("symbol") + ". Wait for it to finish before starting another run.");
 201:         }
 202: 
 203:         String symbol = request.symbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 204:         ReplayDataSource effectiveSource = dataSource == null ? ReplayDataSource.DATABASE : dataSource;
 205:         String baseTestName = request.testName() == null || request.testName().isBlank()
 206:                 ? symbol + " regression" : request.testName().trim();
 207:         String testName = (baseTestName + " [" + effectiveSource.name() + "]").substring(
 208:                 0, Math.min(180, baseTestName.length() + effectiveSource.name().length() + 3));
 209: 
 210:         KeyHolder keyHolder = new GeneratedKeyHolder();
 211:         jdbcTemplate.update(connection -> {
 212:             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement("""
 213:                     INSERT INTO analysis_test_run
 214:                         (test_name, symbol, start_time, end_time, status, progress_percent, current_step)
 215:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'PENDING', 0, 'Queued from Administration')
 216:                     """, Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);
 217:             statement.setString(1, testName);
 218:             statement.setString(2, symbol);
 219:             statement.setTimestamp(3, Timestamp.from(request.startTime()));
 220:             statement.setTimestamp(4, Timestamp.from(request.endTime()));
 221:             return statement;
 222:         }, keyHolder);
 223: 
 224:         Number key = keyHolder.getKey();
 225:         if (key == null) {
 226:             throw new IllegalStateException("Could not create regression test run.");
 227:         }
 228:         long id = key.longValue();
 229:         // FIX-122: persist the business-rule revision separately from data-source/experimental modes.
 230:         jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO fix122_replay_revision(test_run_id,enabled) VALUES (?,?)",
 231:                 id, !Boolean.FALSE.equals(request.fix122Enabled()));
 232:         worker.runAsync(id, effectiveSource);
 233:         return id;
 234:     }

 237:     /**
 238:      * Archives one complete replay run before it can be cleared from the active test tables.
 239:      *
 240:      * IMPORTANT regression-history rule (introduced after the ETHUSDT Trade #2 investigation,
 241:      * Aug-2026): test rows are evidence. We must be able to rerun changed production logic without
 242:      * destroying the old pipeline/evidence/health/trade trace that still needs manual analysis.
 243:      * Proven trades remain separate in proven_analyzed_trade and are never moved or deleted here.
 244:      */
 245:     @Transactional
 246:     public synchronized Map<String, Object> archiveRun(long runId, String reason) {
 247:         Integer active = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=? AND status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')", Integer.class, runId);
 248:         if (active != null && active > 0) {
 249:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Test #" + runId + " is still running and cannot be archived.");
 250:         }
 251:         List<Map<String,Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id,test_name,symbol,start_time,end_time,status FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=?", runId);
 252:         if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Regression test #" + runId + " not found.");
 253:         List<Map<String,Object>> existing = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id FROM regression_test_archive_batch WHERE source_test_run_id=?", runId);
 254:         if (!existing.isEmpty()) return Map.of("archiveBatchId", existing.get(0).get("id"), "sourceTestRunId", runId, "alreadyArchived", true);
 255:         Map<String,Object> r = rows.get(0);

 725:     private void saveGenerationError(long runId, String symbol, String interval, Candle candle, String error) {
 726:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 727:                 INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
 728:                     (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
 729:                      generated_at, latest_price, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)

 731:                 """, runId, symbol, interval, Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),

 735: 
 736:     /**
 737:      * FIX-069: write every field mapped by the TradeSignal JPA entity into trade_signal_test.
 738:      * Reflection is intentional here: whenever production TradeSignal gains another @Column,
 739:      * replay inherits it automatically instead of drifting behind a hand-maintained test schema.
 740:      */
 741:     private void persistTradeSignalTest(long runId, TradeSignal signal, Long sourceSignalId, String generationError) {
 742:         try {
 743:             java.util.List<String> columns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 744:             java.util.List<Object> values = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 745:             columns.add("test_run_id"); values.add(runId);
 746:             // FIX-112D / Replay=Production audit lineage: this ID points only to the
 747:             // exact Production signal for the same symbol + interval + candle_open_time.
 748:             // It is additive Replay metadata and is never an execution authority.
 749:             columns.add("source_signal_id"); values.add(sourceSignalId);
 750:             columns.add("replay_generated"); values.add(1);
 751:             columns.add("generation_error"); values.add(generationError);
 752: 
 753:             for (Field field : TradeSignal.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
 754:                 if ("id".equals(field.getName()) || "createdAt".equals(field.getName())) continue;
 755:                 Column column = field.getAnnotation(Column.class);
 756:                 if (column == null) continue;
 757:                 field.setAccessible(true);
 758:                 Object value = field.get(signal);
 759:                 if (value instanceof Enum<?> e) value = e.name();
 760:                 if (value instanceof Instant instant) value = Timestamp.from(instant);
 761:                 String columnName = column.name();
 762:                 if (columnName == null || columnName.isBlank()) columnName = snakeCase(field.getName());
 763:                 columns.add(columnName);
 764:                 values.add(value);
 765:             }
 766: 
 767:             String placeholders = String.join(",", java.util.Collections.nCopies(values.size(), "?"));
 768:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test (" + String.join(",", columns) + ") VALUES (" + placeholders + ")", values.toArray());
 769:         } catch (Exception e) {
 770:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
 771:         }
 772:     }
 773: 
 774:     private String snakeCase(String value) {
 775:         return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
 776:     }
 777: 
 778:     private long intervalSeconds(String interval) {
 779:         return switch (interval) {
 780:             case "1m" -> 60L;
 781:             case "5m" -> 300L;
 782:             case "1h" -> 3600L;
 783:             default -> 60L;
 784:         };
 785:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java (1-210) =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;

   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;

  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
  73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
  74:         State s = required();
  75:         s.reference = observedAt;
  76:         s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
  77:     }
  78: 
  79:     public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
  80:         State s = required();
  81:         return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
  82:     }
  83: 
  84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
  85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  86:         return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
  90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
 148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
 149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
 150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
 151:         return Optional.of(o);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
 155:         State s = required();
 156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
 157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
 158:         return opportunity;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
 162:         private boolean closed;
 163:         @Override public void close() {
 164:             if (!closed) {
 165:                 state.remove();
 166:                 closed = true;
 167:             }
 168:         }
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     private State required() {
 172:         State s = state.get();
 173:         if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
 174:         return s;
 175:     }
 176: 
 177:     public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}
 178: 
 179:     private static final class State {
 180:         final long runId;
 181:         final List<TradeSignal> signals;
 182:         final Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink;
 183:         final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
 184:         boolean fix122Enabled = true;
 185:         final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
 186:         Instant reference;
 187:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity;
 188:         ReplayPrice latestPrice;
 189:         final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
 190:         State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
 191:             this.runId = runId;
 192:             this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
 193:             this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
 194:             this.logicMode = logicMode;
 195:         }
 196:     }
 197: }

  22:     private final TransactionTemplate write;
  23: 
  24:     public StopLossEvidenceStore(DataSource dataSource, PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
  25:         jdbc = new JdbcTemplate(dataSource);
  26:         jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
  27:         read = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  28:         read.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  29:         read.setIsolationLevel(TransactionDefinition.ISOLATION_READ_COMMITTED);
  30:         read.setReadOnly(true);
  31:         read.setTimeout(2);
  32:         write = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  33:         write.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  34:         write.setTimeout(2);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary latest(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  38:         return read.execute(status -> jdbc.query("""
  39:                 SELECT id, executed_at FROM wallet_trade
  40:                 WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED' AND side='SELL'
  41:                   AND execution_reason='STOP_LOSS' AND executed_at<=?
  42:                 ORDER BY executed_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1
  43:                 """, (rs, row) -> new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(rs.getLong(1),
  44:                 rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()), symbol, Timestamp.from(asOf))
  45:                 .stream().findFirst().orElse(null));
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     public void append(Long runId, String symbol, Long signalId, Instant at, String stage, String payload) {
  49:         // Separate audit transaction survives a caller rollback; it records an evaluation,
  50:         // never asserts that a BUY/SELL actually executed. No FK to wallet rows is acquired.
  51:         write.executeWithoutResult(status -> jdbc.update("""
  52:                 INSERT INTO fix122_evaluation(test_run_id,symbol,signal_id,evaluated_at,stage,payload)
  53:                 VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)
  54:                 """, runId, symbol, signalId, Timestamp.from(at), stage, payload));
  55:     }
  56: 
  57:     public List<Map<String,Object>> replayDetails(long runId) {
  58:         return jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id=? ORDER BY id", runId);
  59:     }
  60: 
  61:     public List<Map<String,Object>> productionDetails(String symbol, Instant from, Instant to) {
  62:         return jdbc.queryForList("""
  63:                 SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id IS NULL AND symbol=?
  64:                 AND evaluated_at>=? AND evaluated_at<=? ORDER BY id
  65:                 """, symbol, Timestamp.from(from), Timestamp.from(to));
  66:     }

  15:  * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
  16:  * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
  17:  * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
  18:  * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
  19:  *
  20:  * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
  21:  * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
  22:  */
  23: @Component
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: @Slf4j
  26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
  27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  28: 
  29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
  30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
  31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
  32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
  33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
  34:         }
  35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
  37:         BigDecimal minimum;
  38:         try {
  39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
  40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
  42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
  43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
  44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
  45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
  46:         }
  47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
  48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
  49:         }
  50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
  54:     }
  55: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/DynamicProfitLockService.java (25-80) =====
  25:  * Adaptive policy:
  26:  *  - derive an entry-quality score from the immutable entry signal score and confidence;
  27:  *  - stronger setups receive more room before protection activates;
  28:  *  - weaker/medium setups protect profits earlier;
  29:  *  - the protected price can only move upward and never loosens after activation.
  30:  *
  31:  * Administration values remain the fallback when entry-quality information is unavailable.
  32:  */

  59:      * it non-executable until progress against the CURRENT TP reaches the configured

 208: 
 209:     private int rawConfidence(
 210:             MarketRegimeAssessment regime,
 211:             MarketContextSnapshot context,
 212:             MultiTimeframeConfluenceResult confluence,
 213:             BtcMarketContextResult btc,
 214:             DerivativesPositioningResult derivatives,
 215:             OrderBookLiquidityResult liquidity
 216:     ) {
 217:         double value = regime.confidence() * 0.25;
 218:         value += (context.dataValid() ? 100 : 0) * 0.15;
 219:         value += confluenceConfidence(confluence.status()) * 0.20;
 220:         value += btcConfidence(btc.contextStatus(), btc.stable()) * 0.15;
 221:         value += liquidityConfidence(liquidity.status(), liquidity.observations()) * 0.12;
 222:         value += derivativesConfidence(derivatives) * 0.08;
 223:         value += sentimentConfidence(context) * 0.10;
 224:         int result = (int) Math.round(value);
 225:         return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, result));
 226:     }
 227: 
 228:     private int confluenceConfidence(ConfluenceStatus status) {
 229:         return switch (status) {
 230:             case STRONG_AGREEMENT -> 100;
 231:             case AGREEMENT -> 90;
 232:             case MIXED -> 60;
 233:             case CONFLICT -> 30;
 234:             case STRONG_CONFLICT -> 10;
 235:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
 236:         };
 237:     }
 238: 
 239:     private int btcConfidence(BtcContextStatus status, boolean stable) {
 240:         int base = switch (status) {
 241:             case CONFIRMED -> 95;
 242:             case NOT_APPLICABLE -> 85;
 243:             case NEUTRAL -> 75;
 244:             case LEARNING -> 50;
 245:             case CONFLICT -> 30;
 246:             case STRONG_CONFLICT -> 10;
 247:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
 248:             case STALE_CONTEXT -> 0;
 249:         };
 250:         return stable ? base : Math.max(0, base - 10);
 251:     }
 252: 
 253:     private int liquidityConfidence(LiquidityContextStatus status, int observations) {
 254:         int base = switch (status) {
 255:             case BULLISH_SUPPORT -> 95;
 256:             case BALANCED, DISABLED -> 85;
 257:             case LEARNING -> 55;
 258:             case INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD -> 25;
 259:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
 260:             case BEARISH_PRESSURE, STOP_EXPOSED -> 35;
 261:             case TARGET_BLOCKED, THIN_LIQUIDITY -> 15;
 262:             case WALL_WEAKENING -> 65;
 263:         };
 264:         if (observations <= 0 && status != LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED) {
 265:             return Math.min(base, 40);
 266:         }
 267:         return base;
 268:     }
 269: 
 270:     private int derivativesConfidence(DerivativesPositioningResult result) {
 271:         int base = switch (result.status()) {
 272:             case FRESH_LONG_BUILDUP, FRESH_SHORT_BUILDUP -> 95;
 273:             case HEALTHY_BULLISH, HEALTHY_BEARISH, BALANCED, NOT_APPLICABLE -> 80;
 274:             case SHORT_COVERING, LONG_LIQUIDATION, LOW_CONVICTION -> 55;
 275:             case LONGS_CROWDED, SHORTS_CROWDED -> 25;
 276:             case LEARNING -> 50;
 277:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
 278:         };
 279:         return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, base + result.confidenceAdjustment()));
 280:     }
 281:

 283:         if (!context.sentimentEnabled()) {
 284:             return 70;
 285:         }

  17: /**
  18:  * FIX-112C: durable normalized Order Book evidence for exact historical Replay.
  19:  * The stored imbalance is the existing signed Production formula in [-1,+1].
  20:  *
  21:  * Persistence is deliberately asynchronous and best-effort. Historical Replay parity may
  22:  * become unavailable if persistence fails, but Production sampling/decisions must remain
  23:  * unaffected by database latency, pool exhaustion, or insert errors.
  24:  */
  25: @Service
  26: public class OrderBookSnapshotService {
  27:     private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(OrderBookSnapshotService.class);
  28: 
  29:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
  30:     private final Executor persistenceExecutor;
  31: 
  32:     public OrderBookSnapshotService(
  33:             JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate,
  34:             @Qualifier("orderBookPersistenceExecutor") Executor persistenceExecutor) {
  35:         this.jdbcTemplate = jdbcTemplate;
  36:         this.persistenceExecutor = persistenceExecutor;
  37:     }
  38: 
  39:     /**
  40:      * FIX-112C parity-safety boundary: enqueue persistence and return immediately to the
  41:      * live collector. Queue saturation is reported and the historical row is sacrificed;
  42:      * it must never execute synchronously on the Production collection thread.
  43:      */
  44:     public void recordAsync(String rawSymbol, Instant observedAt,
  45:                             BigDecimal bestBid, BigDecimal bestAsk, BigDecimal spreadPercent,
  46:                             BigDecimal bidDepth, BigDecimal askDepth, BigDecimal depthImbalance,
  47:                             BigDecimal bidWallPrice, BigDecimal bidWallQuantity,
  48:                             BigDecimal askWallPrice, BigDecimal askWallQuantity,
  49:                             int collectionLatencyMs) {
  50:         try {
  51:             persistenceExecutor.execute(() -> {
  52:                 try {
  53:                     record(rawSymbol, observedAt, bestBid, bestAsk, spreadPercent,
  54:                             bidDepth, askDepth, depthImbalance,
  55:                             bidWallPrice, bidWallQuantity, askWallPrice, askWallQuantity,
  56:                             collectionLatencyMs);
  57:                 } catch (Exception exception) {
  58:                     log.warn("Unable to persist historical order-book snapshot for {} at {}: {}. Live Production sampling is unaffected.",
  59:                             rawSymbol, observedAt, exception.getMessage());
  60:                 }
  61:             });
  62:         } catch (RejectedExecutionException exception) {
  63:             log.warn("Historical order-book persistence queue is full for {} at {}; snapshot was not persisted. Live Production sampling is unaffected.",
  64:                     rawSymbol, observedAt);
  65:         }
  66:     }
  67: 
  68:     private void record(String rawSymbol, Instant observedAt,
  69:                         BigDecimal bestBid, BigDecimal bestAsk, BigDecimal spreadPercent,

  73:                         int collectionLatencyMs) {

  75:         String symbol = rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  76:         // Existing live code expresses spread as percent. Persist bps explicitly.
  77:         BigDecimal spreadBps = spreadPercent == null ? null : spreadPercent.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));

  83:                     collection_latency_ms, source)

 160:                 Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 161:             if(applied==null || applied==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 no applied price evidence in collector-era Replay window; signal-price fallback is not Production reproduction");

 137:         List<NormalizedObservation> observations = orderBookSnapshotService

 139:                 .map(row -> new NormalizedObservation(row.observedAt(),

 144:                         // silently change that established Production behavior during Replay.
 145:                         row.bestBid() == null || row.bestAsk() == null
 146:                                 || row.bestBid().add(row.bestAsk()).signum() <= 0
 147:                                 ? null
 148:                                 : new SnapshotMetrics(
 149:                                         row.bidDepth(), row.askDepth(), row.depthImbalance(),
 150:                                         row.spreadBps() == null ? null : row.spreadBps().divide(ONE_HUNDRED, MC),
 151:                                         row.bidWallPrice(), row.bidWallQuantity(),
 152:                                         row.askWallPrice(), row.askWallQuantity())))
 153:                 .toList();
 154:         if (observations.isEmpty()) {
 155:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.UNAVAILABLE, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 156:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 157:                     false, false, 0,
 158:                     "No persisted Production order-book observation exists for this historical replay window; live data was not substituted.",

 160:         }

 165:     public OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluate(
 166:             String symbol,
 167:             String interval,
 168:             SignalDecision currentDecision,
 169:             boolean entryAllowed,
 170:             BigDecimal currentPrice,
 171:             BigDecimal stopLoss,
 172:             BigDecimal takeProfit,
 173:             Instant evaluatedAt
 174:     ) {
 175:         IntervalPolicy policy = properties.policyFor(interval == null ? "5m" : interval);
 176:         Instant snapshotTime = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
 177:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
 178:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 179:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 180:                     false, false, 0, "Order-book liquidity analysis is disabled.", snapshotTime,
 181:                     policy, 0L);
 182:         }
 183: 
 184:         String normalizedSymbol = normalize(symbol);
 185:         Deque<OrderBookSnapshot> deque = historyBySymbol.get(normalizedSymbol);
 186:         if (deque == null || deque.isEmpty()) {
 187:             collectSafely(normalizedSymbol);
 188:             deque = historyBySymbol.get(normalizedSymbol);
 189:         }
 190:         if (deque == null || deque.isEmpty()) {
 191:             // FIX-091 / Fix 2: in LIVE analysis, zero observations are not proof that liquidity is safe.
 192:             // Hold a fresh entry until the configured sample minimum is reached. Historical replay uses
 193:             // evaluateHistorical(...) and deliberately keeps its separate UNAVAILABLE limitation.
 194:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 195:                     false, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 196:                     false, false, 0, "Live order-book sampling is not ready (0/" + policy.minimumObservations()
 197:                             + "). Fresh entry is held until enough observations are collected.", snapshotTime,
 198:                     policy, 0L);
 199:         }
 200: 
 201:         List<OrderBookSnapshot> snapshots;
 202:         synchronized (deque) {
 203:             Instant windowStart = snapshotTime.minusSeconds(policy.windowSeconds());
 204:             snapshots = deque.stream()
 205:                     .filter(snapshot -> !snapshot.capturedAt().isAfter(snapshotTime))
 206:                     .filter(snapshot -> !snapshot.capturedAt().isBefore(windowStart))
 207:                     .toList();
 208:         }
 209:         if (snapshots.isEmpty()) {
 210:             // FIX-091 / Fix 2: an enabled LIVE order-book service with no observation in the active
 211:             // window is an insufficient-sampling condition, not a neutral PASS.
 212:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 213:                     false, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 214:                     false, false, 0,
 215:                     "No live order-book observation exists inside the " + policy.windowSeconds()
 216:                             + " second window for interval " + interval + "; fresh entry is held until "
 217:                             + policy.minimumObservations() + " observations are available.",
 218:                     snapshotTime, policy, 0L);
 219:         }
 220: 
 221:         // FIX-112C parity preservation: keep every live snapshot in sequence and let the
 222:         // shared evaluator enforce the exact pre-refactor latest-snapshot validity gate.
 223:         // Do not filter individual invalid snapshots here; doing so changed live Production
 224:         // semantics by allowing an older valid snapshot to replace an invalid latest reading.
 225:         List<NormalizedObservation> observations = snapshots.stream()

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java (1-100) =====
   1: package com.crypto.regression.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   5: 
   6: import java.util.ArrayList;
   7: import java.util.List;
   8: import java.util.Objects;
   9: 
  10: /**
  11:  * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
  12:  * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: public class ReplayParityValidator {
  16: 
  17:     public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
  18:         List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
  19:         if (production == null || replay == null) {
  20:             mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
  21:             return new Result(false, mismatches);
  22:         }
  23: 
  24:         same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
  25:         same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
  26:         same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
  27:         same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
  28:         same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
  29:         same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
  30:         same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
  31:         same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
  32:         same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
  33:         same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
  34:         same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
  35:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
  36:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
  37:                 production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
  38:         return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
  42:         if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
  43:             mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
  44:         }
  45:     }
  46: 
  47:     public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
  48: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/AnalysisService.java (140-190) =====
 140:         return analyze(indicator);
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     /**
 144:      * Automatic entry point. It receives the TechnicalIndicator row that was
 145:      * just saved by TechnicalIndicatorService and never recalculates candles.
 146:      */
 147:     @Transactional
 148:     public TradeSignal analyze(TechnicalIndicator indicator) {
 149:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 150:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, Instant.now(), false, false);
 151:         // FIX-153: physical createdAt and persisted mode distinguish live latency from as-of history.
 152:         signal.setSignalMode("LIVE");
 153:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     /**
 157:      * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
 158:      * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
 159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
 160:      *
 161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
 162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
 163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
 164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
 165:      */
 166:     @Transactional
 167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
 168:         if (indicator == null) {
 169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
 170:         }
 171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
 173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
 174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
 175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 176:     }
 177:

 143:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_PATH] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, price={}, " +

 145:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, signal.getInterval(), price,

 148:             }

 151:             // a dynamically extended take-profit.  paper_position historically retained the

 155:             // deliberately NOT a TP-policy change: it only makes both existing exit paths compare

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/RegimeStateService.java (1-90) =====
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.MarketRegime;
   4: import com.crypto.dto.MarketRegimeAssessment;
   5: import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
   6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
   7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   8: 
   9: import java.time.Instant;
  10: import java.util.HashMap;
  11: import java.util.List;
  12: import java.util.Map;
  13: 
  14: /**
  15:  * FIX-091 / Fix 4 + FIX-109: Experimental-Replay-only regime persistence state machine.
  16:  * Production behavior is intentionally untouched until the parity gate is passed.
  17:  * State is keyed by replay run + symbol + interval and advances only on candle timestamps.
  18:  */
  19: @Service
  20: public class RegimeStateService {
  21:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  22:     private final int requiredCandles;
  23:     // Run id is part of the key so concurrent Replay/Test workers cannot reset or contaminate
  24:     // one another. The map contains only Replay state; Production never reads it.
  25:     private final Map<String, RegimeState> replayState = new HashMap<>();
  26: 
  27:     public RegimeStateService(ExecutionReplayScope replayScope,
  28:                               @Value("${analysis.regime.persistence-candles:3}") int requiredCandles) {
  29:         this.replayScope = replayScope;
  30:         this.requiredCandles = Math.max(2, requiredCandles);
  31:     }
  32: 
  33:     public synchronized Decision apply(String symbol, String interval, Instant candleTime,
  34:                                        MarketRegimeAssessment detectedAssessment) {
  35:         if (detectedAssessment == null || !replayScope.active() || !replayScope.experimental()) {
  36:             MarketRegime regime = detectedAssessment == null ? MarketRegime.UNKNOWN : detectedAssessment.regime();
  37:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, regime, null, regime, 0, false);
  38:         }
  39: 
  40:         long runId = replayScope.runId();
  41:         String key = runId + "|" + symbol + "|" + interval;
  42:         RegimeState state = replayState.get(key);
  43:         MarketRegime detected = detectedAssessment.regime();
  44:         if (state == null) {
  45:             state = new RegimeState(detected, null, 0, candleTime);
  46:             replayState.put(key, state);
  47:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, detected, null, detected, 0, false);
  48:         }
  49: 
  50:         MarketRegime confirmed = state.confirmed;
  51:         MarketRegime candidate = state.candidate;
  52:         int count = state.candidateCount;
  53:         boolean promoted = false;
  54: 
  55:         if (detected == confirmed) {
  56:             candidate = null;
  57:             count = 0;
  58:         } else if (detected == candidate) {
  59:             count++;
  60:         } else {
  61:             candidate = detected;
  62:             count = 1;
  63:         }
  64: 
  65:         if (candidate != null && count >= requiredCandles) {
  66:             confirmed = candidate;
  67:             candidate = null;
  68:             count = 0;
  69:             promoted = true;
  70:         }
  71:         replayState.put(key, new RegimeState(confirmed, candidate, count, candleTime));
  72: 
  73:         MarketRegimeAssessment effective = detected == confirmed
  74:                 ? detectedAssessment
  75:                 : new MarketRegimeAssessment(confirmed, detectedAssessment.confidence(),
  76:                     append(detectedAssessment.evidence(), "FIX-091 Replay persistence: detected=" + detected
  77:                             + ", candidate=" + candidate + ", confirmed=" + confirmed
  78:                             + ", candidateCount=" + count + "/" + requiredCandles + "."));
  79:         return new Decision(effective, detected, candidate, confirmed, count, promoted);
  80:     }
  81: 
  82:     private List<String> append(List<String> evidence, String value) {
  83:         java.util.ArrayList<String> result = new java.util.ArrayList<>(evidence == null ? List.of() : evidence);
  84:         result.add(value);
  85:         return List.copyOf(result);
  86:     }
  87: 
  88:     private record RegimeState(MarketRegime confirmed, MarketRegime candidate, int candidateCount, Instant lastCandleTime) {}
  89: 
  90:     public record Decision(MarketRegimeAssessment effectiveAssessment,
