  16: 
  17: /**
  18:  * Best-effort, append-only FIX-154 evidence. It snapshots values then writes
  19:  * on a bounded dedicated executor: an audit insert never nests a database
  20:  * connection inside an execution transaction and never changes a BUY result.
  21:  */
  22: @Slf4j
  23: @Component
  24: public class BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore {
  25:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  26:     private final Executor executor;
  27:     private final Fix154Metrics metrics;
  28:     @Autowired(required = false)
  29:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  30: 
  31:     public BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore(JdbcTemplate jdbc, @Qualifier("fix154AuditExecutor") Executor executor,
  32:                                       Fix154Metrics metrics) {
  33:         this.jdbc = jdbc;
  34:         this.executor = executor;
  35:         this.metrics = metrics;
  36:     }
  37: 
  38:     public void record(TradeSignal reference, String path,
  39:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
  40:                        String finalResult, String reason) {
  41:         // Replay signals are JDBC-generated and have no entity id. Replay parity
  42:         // uses its own evidence tables; never write a misleading Production audit.
  43:         if (reference == null || assessment == null || reference.getId() == null
  44:                 || (replayScope != null && replayScope.active())) return;
  45:         AuditPayload payload = AuditPayload.from(reference, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
  46:         try {
  47:             executor.execute(() -> persist(payload));
  48:         } catch (RejectedExecutionException rejected) {
  49:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
  50:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit queue is full; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
  51:                     path, reference.getId());
  52:         }
  53:     }
  54: 
  55:     private void persist(AuditPayload p) {
  56:         try {
  57:             jdbc.update("""
  58:                     INSERT INTO buy_htf_assessment_audit (
  59:                       reference_signal_id, evaluation_path, evaluated_at, replay_mode,
  60:                       one_hour_signal_id, one_hour_raw_decision, one_hour_effective_decision,
  61:                       one_hour_candle_close_time, one_hour_age_seconds, one_hour_data_quality_valid, one_hour_usability_reason,
  62:                       five_minute_signal_id, five_minute_raw_decision, five_minute_effective_decision,
  63:                       five_minute_candle_close_time, five_minute_age_seconds, five_minute_data_quality_valid, five_minute_usability_reason,
  64:                       confidence_threshold, assessment_band, hard_confirmation_present, max_initial_percent,
  65:                       legacy_veto_result, final_result, final_reason)
  66:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, FALSE, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  67:                     """,
  68:                     p.referenceId, p.path, Timestamp.from(p.evaluatedAt),
  69:                     p.one.id, p.one.decision, p.effectiveOne, timestamp(p.one.candleClose), p.one.ageSeconds, p.one.quality, p.one.usability,
  70:                     p.five.id, p.five.decision, p.effectiveFive, timestamp(p.five.candleClose), p.five.ageSeconds, p.five.quality, p.five.usability,
  71:                     HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD, p.band, p.hardConfirmationPresent, p.maxInitialPercent,
  72:                     p.legacyVeto, p.finalResult, p.reason);
  73:         } catch (Exception e) {
  74:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
  75:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit was not recorded; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
  76:                     p.path, p.referenceId, e);
  77:         }
  78:     }
  79: 
  80:     private static Timestamp timestamp(Instant value) { return value == null ? null : Timestamp.from(value); }
  81: 
  82:     private record Context(Long id, String decision, Instant candleClose, Long ageSeconds, Boolean quality, String usability) {
  83:         static Context from(TradeSignal signal, Instant evaluatedAt) {
  84:             if (signal == null) return new Context(null, null, null, null, null, "MISSING");
  85:             Instant close = deriveCandleClose(signal);
  86:             Long age = close == null ? null : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(close, evaluatedAt).getSeconds());
  87:             return new Context(signal.getId(), signal.getDecision() == null ? null : signal.getDecision().name(), close, age,
  88:                     signal.getDataQualityValid(), Boolean.TRUE.equals(signal.getDataQualityValid()) ? "AVAILABLE" : "QUALITY_UNKNOWN");
  89:         }
  90:     }
  91:     private record AuditPayload(Long referenceId, String path, Instant evaluatedAt, Context one, Context five,
  92:                                 String effectiveOne, String effectiveFive, String band, boolean hardConfirmationPresent,
  93:                                 Integer maxInitialPercent, String legacyVeto, String finalResult, String reason) {
  94:         static AuditPayload from(TradeSignal reference, String path, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
  95:                                  String finalResult, String reason) {
  96:             Instant evaluated = reference.getGeneratedAt() == null ? Instant.now() : reference.getGeneratedAt();
  97:             String legacy = "5M_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code()) || "1H_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code())
  98:                     ? assessment.code() : null;
  99:             return new AuditPayload(reference.getId(), path, evaluated,
 100:                     Context.from(assessment.rawOneHour(), evaluated), Context.from(assessment.rawFiveMinute(), evaluated),
 101:                     assessment.effectiveOneHour().name(), assessment.effectiveFiveMinute().name(), assessment.band().name(),
 102:                     assessment.hardConfirmationPresent(), assessment.maxInitialPercent(), legacy, finalResult, trim(reason));
 103:         }
 104:     }
 105:     private static Instant deriveCandleClose(TradeSignal signal) {
 106:         if (signal.getCandleOpenTime() == null || signal.getInterval() == null) return null;
 107:         long seconds = switch (signal.getInterval()) { case "1m" -> 60; case "5m" -> 300; case "1h" -> 3600; default -> 0; };
 108:         return seconds == 0 ? null : signal.getCandleOpenTime().plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1);
 109:     }
 110:     private static String trim(String value) { return value == null ? null : value.length() <= 1000 ? value : value.substring(0, 1000); }
 111: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java (735-800) =====
 735: 
 736:     /**
 737:      * FIX-069: write every field mapped by the TradeSignal JPA entity into trade_signal_test.
 738:      * Reflection is intentional here: whenever production TradeSignal gains another @Column,
 739:      * replay inherits it automatically instead of drifting behind a hand-maintained test schema.
 740:      */
 741:     private void persistTradeSignalTest(long runId, TradeSignal signal, Long sourceSignalId, String generationError) {
 742:         try {
 743:             java.util.List<String> columns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 744:             java.util.List<Object> values = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 745:             columns.add("test_run_id"); values.add(runId);
 746:             // FIX-112D / Replay=Production audit lineage: this ID points only to the
 747:             // exact Production signal for the same symbol + interval + candle_open_time.
 748:             // It is additive Replay metadata and is never an execution authority.
 749:             columns.add("source_signal_id"); values.add(sourceSignalId);
 750:             columns.add("replay_generated"); values.add(1);
 751:             columns.add("generation_error"); values.add(generationError);
 752: 
 753:             for (Field field : TradeSignal.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
 754:                 if ("id".equals(field.getName()) || "createdAt".equals(field.getName())) continue;
 755:                 Column column = field.getAnnotation(Column.class);
 756:                 if (column == null) continue;
 757:                 field.setAccessible(true);
 758:                 Object value = field.get(signal);
 759:                 if (value instanceof Enum<?> e) value = e.name();
 760:                 if (value instanceof Instant instant) value = Timestamp.from(instant);
 761:                 String columnName = column.name();
 762:                 if (columnName == null || columnName.isBlank()) columnName = snakeCase(field.getName());
 763:                 columns.add(columnName);
 764:                 values.add(value);
 765:             }
 766: 
 767:             String placeholders = String.join(",", java.util.Collections.nCopies(values.size(), "?"));
 768:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test (" + String.join(",", columns) + ") VALUES (" + placeholders + ")", values.toArray());
 769:         } catch (Exception e) {
 770:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
 771:         }
 772:     }
 773: 
 774:     private String snakeCase(String value) {
 775:         return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
 776:     }
 777: 
 778:     private long intervalSeconds(String interval) {
 779:         return switch (interval) {
 780:             case "1m" -> 60L;
 781:             case "5m" -> 300L;
 782:             case "1h" -> 3600L;
 783:             default -> 60L;
 784:         };
 785:     }
 786: 
 787:     private int intervalOrder(String interval) {
 788:         return switch (interval) {
 789:             case "1h" -> 0;
 790:             case "5m" -> 1;
 791:             default -> 2;
 792:         };
 793:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/EntryConsumptionPolicy.java (1-60) =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.execution.domain.EntryConsumptionState;
   4: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletTradeRepository;
   5: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   7: 
   8: /**
   9:  * FIX-112A: consumption belongs to the specific bullish signal, not to the
  10:  * symbol's general position state. A pre-existing position can coexist with a
  11:  * blocked progressive-add signal, so OPEN-position state is not proof that the
  12:  * immediately previous bullish signal actually executed.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  16: public class EntryConsumptionPolicy {
  17:     private final WalletTradeRepository walletTradeRepository;
  18:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  19: 
  20:     public EntryConsumptionState resolve(Long previousSignalId) {
  21:         if (previousSignalId == null) return EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  22:         if (replayScope.active()) {
  23:             return replayScope.entryConsumed(previousSignalId)
  24:                     ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED
  25:                     : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  26:         }
  27:         // Production authority is the immutable executed BUY row keyed to the
  28:         // exact triggering signal. This works for both first entries and adds.
  29:         boolean executed = walletTradeRepository
  30:                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
  31:                         previousSignalId, "BUY", "EXECUTED")
  32:                 .isPresent();
  33:         return executed ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  34:     }
  35: }

Direct source checks confirm several blocking mismatches: the artifact names a nonexistent run table, current Replay publishes rows incrementally (with an audit write that survives rollback), and the current price-event object does not carry the ID/ordering slot the draft requires. I also found a correction to my earlier review: both data-source modes build the generation timeline from the same unfiltered candle lists; the issue is still real but not the mode-specific claim I previously made.

## FINDINGS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** RPR-0.6 is not eligible for dual-agent technical-design approval as the implementation contract. Governance requires the exact approved revision at `agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md`; that file is still RPR-0.4, while RPR-0.6 is separate. RPR-0.6 also defers freezing the dirty worktree until immediately before implementation, although a material edit invalidates approval of the reviewed revision. [AGENTS.md](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:327) · [canonical contract](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:3) · [RPR-0.6](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION_RPR-0.6.md:3)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The boundary registry is factually incomplete. `regression_test_run` named by RPR-0.6 does not exist; the current run table is `analysis_test_run`. Replay creates/purges many additional test, diagnostic, and archive tables, while run detail currently reads forbidden Production `wallet_trade`. [spec](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION_RPR-0.6.md:48) · [run creation](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestService.java:213) · [purge inventory](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestService.java:350) · [Production-wallet chart read](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestService.java:549)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** “Stage privately and publish only on terminal success” is not a sufficiently specified implementation contract. Current Replay publishes `analysis_test_signal` and `trade_signal_test` incrementally; FIX-122 audit writes run in an independent `REQUIRES_NEW` transaction and survive a caller rollback. RPR-0.6 neither registers `RegressionTestService` or `StopLossEvidenceStore` nor defines the staging schema, publish transaction, recovery state machine, or compensation rule for surviving writes. [incremental signal write](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:636) · [test-signal persistence](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:741) · [independent audit transaction](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceStore.java:32)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The isolation design is incomplete. Current Replay reads `wallet_settings`; shared validation and profit-lock services do so as well; the minimum-order policy calls the Binance client. RPR-0.6 identifies some affected services but does not name a capability/port enforcement point that fails closed across all call paths and threads. The present replay scope is `ThreadLocal`, so a future async handoff does not inherit it automatically. [Replay wallet read](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:999) · [shared settings read](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:400) · [HTTP dependency](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java:29) · [thread-bound scope](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:31)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** `ANALYSIS_PARITY` has no defined oracle. The existing validator compares a small unversioned exact field set, and no Java caller was found. RPR-0.6 omits its production eligibility eras, identity key, field-set version, tolerances, mismatch/refusal outcome, and dependency-specific evidence routes. [validator](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java:17) · [RPR M03](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION_RPR-0.6.md:88)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Historical context cannot safely be represented by RPR M03’s generic “stored, recomputed, or refusal” language. With no stored order-book rows, historical Replay returns `UNAVAILABLE` without changing entry permission, whereas live Production returns `INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD` and blocks entry. Order-book persistence is explicitly asynchronous and best-effort. Sentiment has only `observed_at`, not a durable collection/commit time, so an as-of query cannot prove that a row was available to the original decision. [historical/live divergence](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/OrderBookLiquidityService.java:154) · [best-effort persistence](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/OrderBookSnapshotService.java:21) · [sentiment schema](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql:69) · [as-of selection](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/SentimentService.java:117)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** RPR M06 does not specify a reproducible Production event model. Production’s signal path has advisory processing, TP, profit-lock trigger, stop, rebase floor, SELL suppression while the lock is active, then add; live price protection is an independent transaction/path. Replay currently processes price events before its signal loop, but both “exact-price” classification and ordering use only timestamps. The event record does not carry the database ID/ordering slot that RPR requires. [Production signal order](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:113) · [Replay ordering](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:122) · [Replay price sort](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:89) · [price-event model](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:173)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Clock parity remains unspecified. Production live analysis stamps signals with `Instant.now()`, whereas Replay generates them at nominal candle close; Production entry/add resolves price at a later `Instant.now()`. This affects as-of context, signal visibility, and same-boundary timeframe ordering even before considering WebSocket receipt timing. [Production timestamp](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/AnalysisService.java:147) · [Replay timestamp](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:583) · [Production execution-price clock](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:237)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The identity and experimental-mode contracts are incomplete. Fresh Replay signals are unsaved and therefore have null entity IDs; current near-TP and entry-consumption rules explicitly depend on non-null IDs. RPR requires stable IDs but lacks a collision-proof namespace and a complete shortcut inventory. Separately, both current callers open default `PRODUCTION_PARITY`; yet both Replay-only observers currently run in baseline execution. RPR registers only one observer and gives no implementation rule for relabelling or separating these paths. [unsaved signal output](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:616) · [ID omitted from test shape](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:753) · [near-TP ID gate](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.java:119) · [default mode](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:33) · [baseline observer calls](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:100)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** RPR-0.6’s release scope does not account for current FIX-154 source behavior. `TradeExecutionValidationService` now has a higher-timeframe assessment dependency, while the audit store deliberately suppresses its Production audit write during Replay and uses a bounded executor in Production. The design must explicitly register the shared assessment, audit suppression, metrics, and its Replay evidence treatment. [validation dependency](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:51) · [Replay audit suppression](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore.java:38)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE — correction]** My earlier claim that only `DATABASE` mode used an unfiltered generation timeline was wrong. Both modes build the timeline from the same initially loaded candle lists; `DATASET` changes snapshot/lineage access. RPR M02 must therefore qualify closed identities before either mode’s timeline, or explicitly retire/redefine both modes. [shared candle loading](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:128) · [shared timeline](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:550) · [dataset-only closed forward query](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:475)

## EVIDENCE

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The RPR-0.6 SHA-256 is `5C057C181459727E7D62F580F525FDB4392E15BBEAD7A27F5BAA2F3AAADF5A03`, and its declared Git baseline equals current `HEAD` (`0cb6a9a6cc64ba8ac9d33797bcc30af534fd46e3`).

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The worktree is materially dirty, including source, migrations, configuration, tests, documentation, and untracked files. Thus the declared commit alone cannot identify the actual code against which this review was conducted.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The checked-in Jenkins pipeline runs `mvn clean package` and then deploys LIVE, with no deployment approval stage and no automatic rollback. A generic “disable Replay route” does not roll back a defect in shared extracted policies. [Jenkinsfile](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/Jenkinsfile:16)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The fresh-install policy remains contradictory: AGENTS.md treats V103’s collector dependency as a release blocker, while B105 says V103 has no trader-only baseline effect. RPR D02/X11 must choose and prove one supported installation policy rather than merely mention both. [AGENTS.md](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:118) · [B105](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql:4)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** No database, logs, runtime, production systems, credentials, or external websites were accessed in this review.

## OBJECTIONS

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** A revised artifact must be canonical, source/config/worktree-manifest frozen before review, and then PDF-frozen after both reviews—not merely re-hashed before implementation.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Add a complete, authoritative run lifecycle: request/controller/service admission, staging objects, publication transaction, terminal status versus claim label, cancellation/crash recovery, archive/purge treatment, and compensating treatment of independently committed audit rows.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Define a fail-closed virtual-account capability passed through every shared policy, with explicit account/HTTP/database allowlists and cross-thread propagation rules. A `ThreadLocal` alone is not that boundary.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Define the `ANALYSIS_PARITY` certificate: identity key, eligible historical Production signals, versioned fields/tolerances, each dependency’s sole evidence route, refusal codes, and guaranteed exclusion of unavailable/late evidence.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Specify separate price-event and signal-event state machines, decision/fill/visibility clocks, same-timestamp tie-breaking using durable identifiers, price-coverage segments, and explicit fixtures for lock-active SELL/add suppression and stop reason handling.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Complete the registry with affected request/controller/UI, `RegressionTestService`, evidence stores, context services, both experimental observers, ID-dependent policies, FIX-154 components, MySQL test profiles, pipeline, and explicit “unchanged because” entries.

## UNKNOWNS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** I did not verify any environment’s historical configuration, enabled context features, Flyway history, stored event coverage, or actual event-timing logs; this review does not claim those facts.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Whether old windows can earn `ANALYSIS_PARITY` remains unknown until the revised qualifier applies the required availability-time and ordering rules to real evidence.
